- Ordinary AddressGroup.Resources.List/All now classify the entire pinned direct declaration through SDK-owned WithFilter/WithFilters: eight canonical and accepted query attributes, three non-query Body attributes id/tenant_id/addresses and nine reserved list controls. AddressGroup directly inherits Resource without NetworkResource or TagMixin. Query name/description/project_id are server-only; no exact local name/project predicate, implicit GET or caller builder is added. Generated leaf and cached Network/NetworkV2 facades share the descriptor.
  Tests: `api/address_group_list_filters_test.go:TestAddressGroupListFiltersEntireQueryDescriptor`, `api/address_group_list_filters_test.go:TestAddressGroupListFiltersRawBodyDescriptorAndPresence`, `connectiontest/connection_address_group_filters_test.go:TestConnectionAddressGroupFiltersSharedFacadesAndRawTenant`.
- Semantic query values encode scalar strings, lowercase booleans and original JSON-number text; flat scalar arrays preserve repeated order while omitting null elements. Null and empty arrays emit no URL values but retain key presence. Unknown semantic names and their encoding errors are discarded; selected maps/nested query arrays fail. The deprecated tenant_id remains a local property: project_id's response descriptor alias does not introduce a tenant_id query alias.
  Tests: `api/address_group_list_filters_test.go:TestAddressGroupListFiltersEntireQueryDescriptor`, `api/address_group_list_filters_test.go:TestAddressGroupListFiltersLazyPreflightAndNamespaceCollisions`, `connectiontest/connection_address_group_filters_test.go:TestConnectionAddressGroupFiltersSharedFacadesAndRawTenant`, `resource/filters_test.go:TestSemanticFiltersCanonicalPrecedencePresenceAndExactQueryEncoding`.
- All three local selectors read original row JSON after native extraction. Literal raw id presence/null/empty stays independent of native typed identity. tenant_id has no fallback to project_id and preserves exact large numbers and unknown nested objects despite being absent from the native model. Missing/null match null, empty strings and arrays remain distinct; arrays compare complete ordered length and elements without CIDR normalization, and objects recursively match subsets of nonempty actual objects. Decimal equality is exact and booleans remain distinct from numbers.
  Tests: `api/address_group_list_filters_test.go:TestAddressGroupListFiltersRawBodyDescriptorAndPresence`, `connectiontest/connection_address_group_filters_test.go:TestConnectionAddressGroupFiltersSharedFacadesAndRawTenant`, `resource/body_record_test.go:TestBodyRecordPairingPreservesOriginalRowsAndOwnsFields`, `resource/body_record_test.go:TestBodyRecordFieldNormalizationPresenceAndCauses`, `internal/jsonfilter/filter_test.go:TestMatchFiltersArraysCompareExactly`, `internal/jsonfilter/filter_test.go:TestMatchFiltersExactDecimalNumbersAndHugeExponents`, `internal/jsonfilter/filter_test.go:TestMatchFiltersDeliberatelyDistinguishesBooleansAndNumbers`, `api/network_body_filters_test.go:TestNetworkBodyFiltersNativeValueSemantics`.
- Matching no longer projects the native Addresses slice: a raw null address element stays null for comparison although the returned native []string decodes that element as an empty string. Returned rows still use the unchanged five-field native model, including independent ProjectID, rather than filter values or a synthetic raw model. Entire address arrays missing/null both match null; ordered values, length and empty arrays remain explicit. Native id/name/description/project_id string types and addresses string-array decoding remain authoritative.
  Tests: `api/address_group_list_filters_test.go:TestAddressGroupListFiltersRawBodyDescriptorAndPresence`, `api/address_group_list_filters_test.go:TestAddressGroupListFiltersControlsAndNativeWholePageFailures`, `api/network_body_filters_test.go:TestNetworkBodyFiltersLocalWireAndNativeSurfaces`.
- Options own nested JSON/query snapshots for independent concurrent reuse. Individual semantic options are last-wins; bulk replace/nil/empty clear affect only semantic state and validate only final selected values, allowing superseded encoding errors to disappear. Same-wire semantic/raw/name/page-size and same-Body semantic/explicit destinations fail lazily before HTTP, including null/empty/equal values. Reserved controls, selected invalid values, unsupported bindings and canceled contexts yield terminal preflight errors. Raw query and explicit Body namespaces remain independent when their destination kinds differ.
  Tests: `api/address_group_list_filters_test.go:TestAddressGroupListFiltersSnapshotsReplacementAndConcurrentReuse`, `api/address_group_list_filters_test.go:TestAddressGroupListFiltersLazyPreflightAndNamespaceCollisions`, `connectiontest/connection_address_group_filters_test.go:TestConnectionAddressGroupFiltersPreflightAndNativeNameHint`, `resource/filters_test.go:TestSemanticFiltersSnapshotsConcurrentReuseAndServerOnlyName`, `resource/filters_test.go:TestSemanticFiltersValidateOnlyFinalSelectedValues`, `resource/filters_test.go:TestSemanticFiltersRejectSelectedErrorsAndReservedControlsBeforeIteration`, `resource/filters_test.go:TestSemanticFiltersNamespaceCollisionsAndIndependentClear`, `resource/body_filters_test.go:TestCollectionBodyFiltersOptionApplicationOwnsRawBytes`, `resource/body_filters_test.go:TestCollectionBodyFiltersSnapshotsBulkAliasesAndConcurrentReuse`.
- Native whole-page extraction precedes raw matching and cap consumption, so malformed known fields after a cap remain terminal with native decode causes. A null row consumed by the Body lane is invalid, while query-only null rows preserve the native zero model and an empty object remains valid. After extraction succeeds a cap or early break can avoid consuming a later null row or continuation. MaxItems counts raw rows before local Body/name predicates without refill or an inferred wire limit; first-page and break stop before continuation, and All returns nil on terminal errors.
  Tests: `api/address_group_list_filters_test.go:TestAddressGroupListFiltersControlsAndNativeWholePageFailures`, `resource/body_record_test.go:TestBodyRecordPairingRejectsUnprovenShapeAndCount`, `resource/body_record_test.go:TestCollectionBodyRecordLaneIsSelectedOnlyForPreparedFilters`, `resource/body_filters_test.go:TestCollectionBodyFiltersRunAfterRawCapsOnBothIteratorPaths`, `api/network_body_filters_test.go:TestNetworkBodyFiltersRawControlsNameAndConsumerBreak`, `api/network_body_filters_test.go:TestNetworkBodyFiltersNativePageErrorsAndLateCancellation`.
- Ordinary native listing retains HTTP200/204/300 and rejects201. A successfully parsed204 is empty, while empty application/json204 retains native EOF. address_groups_links rel=next supplies continuation; top-level links.next alone is ignored, matching duplicates remain ordinary list rows and foreign advertised URLs retain native following. Later HTTP/decode/foreign errors, cycles and cancellation remain terminal. Configured headers/current microversion, shared ProviderClient/live token, HTTP middleware, context and ResourceBase reverse prefix remain in use across pages.
  Tests: `api/address_group_list_filters_test.go:TestAddressGroupListFiltersPagingLiveSourceAndNativeStatus`, `connectiontest/connection_address_group_filters_test.go:TestConnectionAddressGroupFiltersSharedFacadesAndRawTenant`, `api/network_body_filters_test.go:TestNetworkBodyFiltersNativePageErrorsAndLateCancellation`.
- Ordinary raw WithQuery preserves complete wire queries, including id/tenant_id/addresses/status/vendor keys, separately from semantic classification. Existing WithName retains a server hint plus exact local predicate; semantic queried name remains server-only. WithStatus stays unsupported because the native model has no Status, while raw status stays wire-only on both normal and Body lanes. Generated native typed List and existing FindIdentity keep their separate signatures/raw query policy and do not acquire ambient semantic or Body options.
  Tests: `api/address_group_list_filters_test.go:TestAddressGroupListFiltersNativeAndIdentitySurfaceIsolation`, `connectiontest/connection_address_group_filters_test.go:TestConnectionAddressGroupFiltersPreflightAndNativeNameHint`, `api/network_body_filters_test.go:TestNetworkBodyFiltersLocalWireAndNativeSurfaces`.
- Generation requires the checked-in AddressGroup manifest and a live audited -openstacksdk-source checkout. Five exact source SHA files are validated before isolated AST-only extraction and full manifest comparison, including parser minor version, direct Resource C3 MRO, pagination defaults, declared query/Body classification, unknown discard and canonical precedence. Sixteen AST anchors independently prove project_id's descriptor alias, deprecated tenant_id, list-typed addresses and inherited ID/accessor behavior; no SDK imports or fabricated tenant query alias are used. Missing/stale source or manifest fails closed and prior four manifests retain their exact bytes.
  Tests: `internal/cmd/sdkgen/address_group_filters_test.go:TestAddressGroupPythonFilterManifestKeepsQueryAndLocalTenantIdentity`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/address_group_filters_test.go:TestAddressGroupPythonFilterPinnedSourceRequiresLiveAliasAndCoercionProof`.
- The raw semantic plan requires exact native AddressGroup5/ListOpts9/Link2 field/type/tag shapes, no own model methods, the value query-builder signature and own linked-page/extractor contracts. Fourteen receiver-qualified native/root/pagination declaration hashes guard routes, native extraction, original GetBody pairing and UseNumber precision. Emission retains complete repeat/nil/empty query maps on both lanes, raw status policy, prior audited bindings and distinct explicit-Body/semantic inventory. Identity opt-ins remain twenty, Body bindings eight and semantic bindings five.
  Tests: `internal/cmd/sdkgen/address_group_filters_test.go:TestAddressGroupBodyFilterNativeContractRejectsModelBuilderPagerAndSourceDrift`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/address_group_filters_test.go:TestAddressGroupSemanticFilterEmissionPreservesRawFieldsAndExistingBindings`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/address_group_filters_test.go:TestAddressGroupRawDependencyLoaderPreservesNumbersAndNativeLinkHelpers`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/address_group_filters_test.go:TestAddressGroupBodyFilterAcceptsActualCompiledNativeSchemasAndOwnMethods`.
- Lazy bodyless GET at /address-groups follows the actual Source server QueryParameters including fields unchanged, list-only local id/tenant_id/addresses filters, no identity/name hint and discarded unknown values without MarshalJSON. Source list Accept defaults application/json over captured Accept, explicit option Accept overrides. Existing exact six descriptors/project alias/list conversion/null defaults/unknown extensions are reused with independent Resource/Wire/whole Envelope/Header/status channels; no tag/NetworkResource headers are synthesized.
  Tests: `network/v2/extensions/security/addressgroups/python_list_records_contracts_test.go:TestPythonAddressGroupListSourceProjectionQueryAndEvidence`.
- Frozen common Engine.List/REST pager supplies plural/body/RFCLink/versioned-path/short-page marker continuation, retained actual scalar JSON marker conversion, complete pagination, raw caps before local filters, single page/break without next request and late native/page errors. SourceFindPolicy opt-in uses binary64 next truthiness; ±1e-400 stop cleanly while truthy nonstring next retains exact actual whole page proof. Dictionary next links are an explicit Go improvement; changed origin/path/filter/cycle are errors.
  Tests: `network/v2/extensions/security/addressgroups/python_list_records_contracts_test.go:TestPythonAddressGroupListPaginationCapsNextTruthinessAndLateErrors`.
- Actual final200..399 representations, strict other native status errors, singular plural-object/array schema, missing/null/malformed/nonobject/UTF8 page faults, constructor cls null/false plus connection/microversion/_synchronized errors and Source recursive dict truthiness remain proven. Exact-decimal local numeric comparison is separately bounded from Python binary64 truthiness and kept explicitly documented.
  Tests: `network/v2/extensions/security/addressgroups/python_list_records_contracts_test.go:TestPythonAddressGroupListConstructorDecodeAndLocalFilterFailures`.
- Read+Close/cancel cause/source-changed-and-restored-on-Close on accepted/rejected list responses preserve every physical cause, original whole body/Header/status, close exactly once, perform exactly one actual HTTP and never become empty/success/retry.
  Tests: `network/v2/extensions/security/addressgroups/python_list_records_contracts_test.go:TestPythonAddressGroupListPhysicalFaultsRetainActualProofWithoutReplay`.
- Native retry cannot alter/delete/Omit selected protocol version or add absent/foreign version, auth/framing or JSON to the bodyless GET. Original native503 proof and optional callback cause stay visible with no resend. Ordinary new retry Header/live-token/reauthentication changes remain native; exactly owned route/query is reused.
  Tests: `network/v2/extensions/security/addressgroups/python_list_records_contracts_test.go:TestPythonAddressGroupListRetryHeadersVersionAndReauthOwnership`.
- Source capture precedes callbacks; bulk maps/raw JSON/headers/version/options and retained callback results are independently owned, invalid context/options/control/header/JSON/paging/source values fail before actual HTTP with causes, unknown bulk raw values are discarded. Immutable options produce independent records under8 concurrent calls.
  Tests: `network/v2/extensions/security/addressgroups/python_list_records_contracts_test.go:TestPythonAddressGroupListOptionsPreflightSnapshotsAndConcurrentReuse`.
- Direct List per-call explicit version applies to every list page, replacing captured matching version headers on only the private operation. Explicit empty means version absence; original selected client/default/header stay unchanged and no class discovery is added.
  Tests: `network/v2/extensions/security/addressgroups/python_list_records_contracts_test.go:TestPythonAddressGroupListExplicitVersionOwnsEveryPageAndOriginalHeaders`.

Python/Go differences:

- Complete declared classification is provided through concrete SDK-owned semantic and explicit Body options. Query name/description/project_id stay server-only and tenant_id remains local, distinct from project_id's descriptor alias. WithName has its own hint and local predicate; raw query keys are explicit wire extensions and semantic filtering does not change typed List or FindIdentity.
- Individual semantic options use last-wins targets and bulk replace/clear affects only semantic state. Only final selected values are validated, while explicit Body errors keep their own behavior. Equal/null/empty same-destination collisions fail rather than permissive kwargs merging. Query scalar arrays become repeated values, null elements are omitted, booleans are lowercase, JSON numbers retain text and unknown semantic names/errors are discarded.
- Matching retains original id/tenant_id/addresses values and exact large unknown tenant JSON; returned values remain the five-field native model. Raw null array elements differ from the native empty-string projection. Python addresses(type=list) may wrap non-list response values into lists; the native decoder instead rejects incompatible known fields before matching and before a cap.
- Exact JSON equality distinguishes bools from numbers and strings from numbers. Recursive object subsets preserve the pinned empty-actual-object nonmatch; nonobject actual values are nonmatches rather than potential Python attribute-access errors. Arrays require complete order/length/element equality and do not normalize CIDRs or apply object-subset matching inside arrays.
- Native200/204/300, plural-links continuation and foreign URL following remain intact. Empty application/json204 can fail with native EOF. Local caps count raw rows like the pinned implementation but do not infer its wire limit; first-page/break stop before continuation and All discards partial rows on terminal failures. No same-origin/fixed-query or synthesized marker policy is added.
- Raw Body selectors run only for consumed selected fields after native whole-page decoding. A consumed null row is invalid on the Body lane; query-only null rows preserve the native zero model. SDK comparisons do not replace Python Resource defaults/alias/unknown response state or provide a tracked/cache lifecycle. Source/manifest/native drift fails closed without activating additional native resources or identity capabilities.
- One bounded direct Source list callable mapping reuses existing AddressGroupFindRecord (public AddressGroupListRecord alias), verified six Body/query tables and frozen common neutronfind.Engine.List with SourceFindPolicy=true. Existing Find/native Get/Create/Update/Delete/actions are not changed or recounted.
- Go JSON-domain inputs/results and concrete options own source/policy/route/version/header/paging state. Full Python mutable Resource dirty/cache/session/Adapter/Connection/computed location and object subclass behavior remain global tasks, rather than being claimed through one list row. Literal observed unknown location follows existing record model; complete Connection-derived location calculation is not synthesized.
- Shared local equality retains exact decimal JSON numeric values, not Python JSON binary64 equality; integer9007199254740992 versus filter9007199254740993.0 is explicitly different. Scalar dict/next/pagination truthiness does use Source binary64 underflow/overflow. Raw Wire/Envelope numbers remain exact. Shared Requests repr/Unicode numeric representation runtime boundaries stay documented.
- Bulk maps sort filter keys while individual options preserve insertion order. SDK constructor/route/session/cache/Computed-location controls, deprecated jmespath_filters and __conflicting_attrs overload are rejected instead of being silently mapped into Body/server query; mutable/restoration/JMESPath compatibility remains separately tracked.
- Owned standard links:{next:URL} compatibility extends pinned Source dict-loop behavior. Origin/path/filter/cycle changes are rejected; scalar markers must expand to one nonempty transport value. Limit/max_items are finite nonnegative Go integers and typed pagination is bool; raw paginated follows JSON truthiness, rather than arbitrary Python object controls.
- Go handles final service representations200..399 and retains other actual native status evidence; arbitrary Python Adapter returning final1xx is outside the engine policy. Explicit empty per-call version means absent version headers and immutable retry contracts are library-owned Go behavior.
- Stock Neutron27.0.0 get_address_group policy permits admin/owning-project reader or shared groups and has no service-only branch. Source unknown shared/tags/all_projects query does not grant roles. SDK fixtures do not establish live endpoint/backend/policy acceptance.
- Go fixes the SDK-owned location view to null while preserving actual values in Wire. Source Computed location may accept explicit row kwargs in LIST construction; mutable Connection-derived location remains unresolved under SDK-R1.

- The explicit Source-shaped list lane now accepts JSON falsey, true, negative and fractional max_items values, counts raw rows before local filtering, and defers truthy nonnumeric comparison errors until a nonempty raw row; falsey/container and raw limit hints preserve their separate wire forms.

- The cap is checked before each next raw-row constructor. Exact-page cap boundaries may fetch a following page and expose its HTTP/read/Close/context failure; malformed rows beyond the cap are ignored and filtered rows count without refill. Legacy Find/native/typed pager policies remain separate.
  Tests: `network/v2/networks/python_raw_max_items_test.go:TestPythonNetworkRawMaxItemsExactPageBoundaryObservesNextResponse`, `network/v2/networks/python_raw_max_items_test.go:TestPythonNetworkRawMaxItemsCountsFilteredRowsAndStopsBeforeConstructor`.
- ProxyListRecords (Proxy.networks) and ProxyCloudListRecords: Raw limit values reach the wire with Requests spelling (False, -1, 1.5, x, repeated values) and Resource.list keeps the original limit for its loop: only a Python-truthy limit performs the marker GET after a nonempty page without a next link, so false/0/0.0/""/null send one GET. Integer max_items equal to one whole page is compared before the next raw row, so the marker GET is performed and its failure is observed; raw true/negative/fractional caps follow the same before-row comparison. base_path="" never enters Resource.list's while loop: no HTTP and no KSA microversion normalization, so an invalid raw microversion is unobserved and the result is empty; a nonempty route still rejects it before HTTP.

Python/Go differences:

- Go uses concrete WithFilter/WithFilters and explicit WithBodyFilter options with frozen values instead of Python keyword arguments and mutable Resource objects. Semantic names follow Python properties; raw subnets/vlan_qinq/vlan_transparent are accepted only by explicit Body filters or raw wire query. Body bulk alias collisions reject; semantic bulk query canonical keys win by presence and sequential options are last-wins. Namespace conflicts reject deterministically.
- Local comparisons use original row JSON after native whole-page decoding, retaining selected large numbers, timestamp spelling and null array elements that native return projections normalize or omit. Returned models remain native. Query-only null rows retain native zero models; consumed null rows with Body conditions reject, while empty object rows are valid.
- Boolean response normalization follows JSON truthiness and preserves missing/null instead of false. Go exact decimals preserve tiny nonzero truthiness where Python float parsing can underflow. Caller filters are not coerced: Go distinguishes bool/number and string/number equality. Exact-integral response policy rejects fractions/bools instead of Python integer truncation/bool-as-int, and accepts signed decimal integer strings rather than digit-only/zero fallback.
- Python list descriptors wrap non-list values; Go selected raw JSON does not wrap. Arrays compare complete ordered values, standalone dictionaries use recursive subset matching on nonempty objects, and unknown extension fields do not become returned native model fields. Exact timestamp text differs even when instants are equivalent.
- Known query scalar arrays become repeated wire values without Python formatting arbitrary containers. Nil/empty arrays omit URL values but preserve key presence. Unknown semantic names are discarded, while raw WithQuery preserves caller wire names/extensions/repeated data and does not classify aliases or Body fields.
- max_items caps raw rows locally without Python Resource.list automatic limit hints. Native accepted 200/204/300, JSON-empty-204 EOF, full-page typed extraction and advertised networks_links/foreign following are retained. Explicit first page and early break avoid continuation; typed native List, FindIdentity and other CRUD are separate surfaces.
- New record API is a sibling to existing typed List/All and semantic Collection APIs. It implements Source owned descriptors, whole-page/private receipts, paging, per-call controls and actual Proxy generator JMES results; prior exact-decimal/native typed-detection differences describe those existing surfaces and do not override the new record contracts.
- Record JSON is immutable owned view rather than lazy mutable Python Resource. Every declared field is projected before row return, so an unaccessed malformed descriptor can fail earlier. Unknown Body extensions are preserved; location is still owned null rather than Python current_location or computed constructor synthesis. Shared SDK Resource/cache/lifecycle parity remains independently tracked.
- Source complete kwargs behavior is implemented in the JSON domain with concrete header string-map and microversion/base-path string types. None-valued headers/adapter/subclass injection, runtime non-JSON objects and arbitrary URI percent-format are not reintroduced. Default microversion has no Network class maximum, and unknown LIST skip_cache is ignored exactly rather than inventing a per-call cache override.
- Go collection continuation retains fixed service origin/path/filter/cursor guards. Per-call base_path selects a service-relative collection; typed ListRecords/AllRecords choose the default collection for an empty path, while ProxyListRecords and ProxyCloudListRecords follow Source and make no request. Source arbitrary adapter retargeting and informational final100..199 remain explicit differences. Original selected clients, live tokens and approved clean retries retain source ownership.
- Typed ListRecords/AllRecords yield resources only; truthy-string JMES calls use ProxyListRecords with arbitrary Value and optional JSON. Fully JSON null is JSON null, generator-containing values keep their opaque generator identity and no fabricated Wire/HTTP metadata. This mirrors the pinned generator behavior, which differs from cloud expression filtering over collected arrays.
- Source general numeric equality is opt-in through PythonLoadedEquality for this new profile: float tokens use CPython binary64 rounding/nonfinite overflow/underflow, integer tokens remain arbitrary precision, nested bool-number equality is preserved. Raw actual bytes are not rewritten. The rest of the repository is not silently migrated.
- Neutron27 stock get_network allows project-reader/service/shared/external/advsvc branches with separate ADMIN provider/segments attributes. SDK does not elevate access or infer all-project scope; missing user attributes retain declared defaults. Fixture/race/source/main evidence does not prove authenticated deployment policy/extension/backend behavior.
- Earlier typed/ProxyListRecords lane only: Record JSON is immutable owned view rather than lazy mutable Python Resource. Every declared field is projected before row return, so an unaccessed malformed descriptor can fail earlier. Unknown Body extensions are preserved; location is still owned null rather than Python current_location or computed constructor synthesis. Shared SDK Resource/cache/lifecycle parity remains independently tracked.
- Earlier typed/ProxyListRecords lane only: The earlier typed lane has concrete header string-map and microversion/base-path string types; the new raw Source cloud lane accepts audited numeric/iterable microversion and deferred formals. None-valued headers/adapter/subclass injection, runtime non-JSON objects and arbitrary URI percent-format are not reintroduced. Default microversion has no Network class maximum, and unknown LIST skip_cache is ignored exactly rather than inventing a per-call cache override.
- Earlier typed/ProxyListRecords lane only: Go collection continuation retains fixed service origin/path/filter/cursor guards. Per-call base_path selects a service-relative collection; typed ListRecords/AllRecords choose the default collection for an empty path, while ProxyListRecords and ProxyCloudListRecords follow Source and make no request. Source arbitrary adapter retargeting and informational final100..199 remain explicit differences. Original selected clients, live tokens and approved clean retries retain source ownership.
- SDK-R1: Go returns owned RawResource/receipt views instead of a mutable Python Resource and cache/lifecycle object. Declared descriptors are projected before row return and may fail earlier for an unaccessed malformed property. Actual Wire/Envelope/header/status are never forged from normalized values; runtime Resource.microversion attribute/object identity remain generic Resource parity scope.
- SDK-C1: JSON bytes retain exact integers and raw proof; local comparisons use audited CPython-loaded float/bool/int policy. Non-JSON custom objects, nonfinite JSON runtime values and exact unrestricted Python representation/Unicode/marshal behavior remain independent generic contracts. Go wraps structured errors while preserving actual response and physical/context causes.
- SDK-S1: source origin, service-relative path/filter/continuation guards and concrete transport options retain the original selected client. Arbitrary adapter/class injection, URI percent-mapping retargeting, foreign/query/fragment/traversal base paths are restricted; existing typed/legacy list APIs retain their baseline rather than receiving hidden Source flags.
- New ProxyCloudListRecords raw WithCloudRecordListQuery preserves Resource formal validation timing and original/restored binding behavior. It is the Source-connected lane; older WithRecordListQuery/ProxyListRecords keep the prior eager concrete control contract. Informational final100..199 and Source continuation-link policy apply only to the new cloned profile.
- Source cloud list() turns dict results into insertion-ordered keys; Go uses lexical keys and has an explicit z,a→a,z assertion. Opaque nested generators remain in Values with JSON=nil; ordinary outputs have Networks/Values/JSON. Typed-only callers can select Networks while arbitrary JMES callers inspect Values.
- Neutron27 stock get_network project-reader/service/shared/external/advsvc paths do not guarantee ADMIN-only provider/segments attributes or all-project visibility. SDK injects current-location facts but does not elevate access. Actual pinned Source/AST/fixture/race/build receipts and live authenticated deployment evidence remain distinct.
- Raw limit spelling and Python truthiness, raw max_items caps and the cap-equals-page marker GET are implemented on the Source-shaped Proxy lanes (shared opt-in Source list controls); typed ListRecords/AllRecords and the semantic Collection lanes keep strict Go integer limit/max_items preflight.
- Raw max_items behavior and exact-boundary cap timing are owned only by the explicit Source-shaped list path; historical typed/native contracts above retain their original boundaries.
- Only standard finite JSON numbers are accepted: NaN, Infinity and overflowing literals such as 1e400 return ErrUnsupported with the original response, where Python's json loads them as floats, and a latest microversion must be written as "latest"; see Deliberate runtime differences in docs/sdk-support-ledger.md.

- Canonical is_shared/any_tags/not_tags/not_any_tags transpose to shared/tags-any/not-tags/not-tags-any. Scalar strings, lowercase booleans and original JSON-number text encode without response-descriptor coercion. Selected query objects/nested arrays fail, while unknown semantic names and their encoding errors are discarded.

- Local id/tenant_id/prefixes/created_at/updated_at read original row JSON after successful native whole-page extraction. Literal missing/null ID or tenant matches null, distinct from a present empty string; tenant never falls back to project_id and the project response alias does not create a tenant query alias. Full prefix arrays compare ordered length/elements and preserve null elements for matching while the returned native []string uses an empty string. No CIDR normalization is added. Timestamp conditions compare original text, so equivalent instants with different text differ, while returned CreatedAt/UpdatedAt retain native time.Time decoding.

- The five selected integer response descriptors default_prefix_length/maximum_prefix_length/minimum_prefix_length/default_quota/revision_number use exact-integral raw normalization. Integral decimal/exponent numbers and signed decimal integer strings compare mathematically when permitted by preceding native decoding; large original prefix numbers remain distinct from rounded native float64/int projection, and filter values are not coerced. Selected fractional prefix responses are terminal invalid-option errors rather than Python int truncation; unselected native fractional prefixes retain native truncation. Missing/null quota/revision can match null, while missing/null native prefix fields fail native decoding first.
  Tests: `api/subnet_pool_list_filters_test.go:TestSubnetPoolListFiltersRawPresenceIntegersAndTimestamps`, `api/subnet_pool_list_filters_test.go:TestSubnetPoolListFiltersControlsAndNativeWholePageFailures`, `connectiontest/connection_subnet_pool_filters_test.go:TestConnectionSubnetPoolFiltersSharedFacadesAndRawDescriptors`, `resource/body_record_test.go:TestBodyRecordFieldNormalizationPresenceAndCauses`, `internal/jsonfilter/filter_test.go:TestMatchFiltersExactDecimalNumbersAndHugeExponents`, `internal/jsonfilter/filter_test.go:TestMatchFiltersDeliberatelyDistinguishesBooleansAndNumbers`.
- Options own deep JSON/query snapshots for independent concurrent reuse. Bulk nil/empty replacement clears only semantic state and validates final selected values, allowing overwritten errors to disappear; explicit Body/raw-query namespaces remain separate. Same-wire semantic/raw/name/page-size and same-Body semantic/explicit destinations fail lazily before HTTP even for null/empty/equal values. Reserved controls, selected invalid encodings, unsupported descriptors and canceled contexts yield terminal preflight errors. Local Body id/tenant conditions and raw wire id/tenant parameters are independent destinations.

- Native extraction of the entire page precedes raw matching and cap consumption. All three native prefix lengths must be numbers or Atoi-compatible strings: missing/null/bool/object/invalid-string values, null rows and empty objects fail even on the query-only lane or after a cap. Known quota/revision/string/bool/tag/prefix-array/timestamp errors keep native causes; mixed incompatible timestamp modes also fail. Only after extraction succeeds can raw cap or break avoid a later selected fractional normalization or continuation. MaxItems counts raw rows before local Body/name predicates without refill or invented wire limit, and All discards partial rows on terminal errors.

- Ordinary native listing preserves HTTP 200/204/300 and rejects 201. A successfully parsed 204 is empty while empty application/json 204 retains native EOF. subnetpools_links rel=next supplies continuation; top-level next/links.next alone is ignored and duplicate matching rows remain ordinary rows. Foreign advertised URLs retain native following. Later HTTP/decode/foreign failures, cycles and cancellation are terminal. Configured headers/current microversion, original ProviderClient/live token, middleware, context and ResourceBase reverse prefix remain in use across pages.

- Raw WithQuery remains a literal wire extension, including id/tenant_id/prefixes/raw prefix spellings/status/vendor parameters; semantic raw prefix spellings remain unknown rather than invented property aliases. WithName retains server hint plus exact local predicate while semantic queried name is server-only. WithStatus stays unsupported on the statusless model. Native typed List, FindIdentity and AddPrefixes/RemovePrefixes keep their existing signatures and request behavior; ordinary list filters do not leak into their GET or prefix-operation requests.

- Generation requires the checked-in SubnetPool manifest and live audited -openstacksdk-source checkout. Seven source SHA files are verified before isolated AST-only extraction and full manifest comparison, including parser minor version, exact Resource+TagMixinNetwork C3 MRO, inherited limit/marker defaults, tag query expansion, all local classification, unknown discard and canonical query precedence. Thirty-three AST anchors independently prove the five integer descriptors, prefixes list descriptor, project alias/deprecated tenant, untyped timestamps, inherited ID/accessor/default behavior and local-dictionary filtering. SubnetPool.name is ordinary rather than alternate_id; no SDK import, NetworkResource hierarchy or tenant query is fabricated. Missing/stale source or manifest fails closed, and the prior six manifests stay exact.

- The raw semantic plan requires exact native SubnetPool 18/ListOpts 22/Link 2 field/type/tag shapes, sole pointer model/time-wrapper decoders, the sole value query-builder and exact interface signature, and native linked-page/extractor/result contracts. Twenty-two receiver-qualified native/root/pagination declaration hashes protect routes, native extraction and NoZ/RFC3339 decoding, page creation/GetBody/UseNumber pairing, accepted page codes and handler/continuation ordering. Two constants protect resourcePath and the NoZ time layout. Emission retains repeat/nil/empty whole query maps on both lanes, raw status, the prior six audited bindings and distinct explicit Body/semantic inventory metadata. Identity opt-ins remain twenty, Body bindings eight and semantic bindings seven; actual compiled native export schemas are also checked.

- Update sends only raw supplied attrs in subnetpool envelope, resolves canonical/wire aliases in option order, preserves null/false/zero/unknown attrs and sparse member seed while retaining independent literal Wire/Envelope/Header/status. No attrs is a no-HTTP commit, tags default to [] and missing Body fields become null; this Resource has no NetworkResource conditional Header descriptors.

- Member kwargs remain original query plus constructor seed; fallback/direct lists transpose only Source declared query aliases, canonical keys override wire aliases even when null, known nonserver canonical Body attrs filter descriptor views, unknown list attrs are discarded, and all_projects is consumed without granting access. fields list values remain literal, tags and repeated query values preserve Requests JSON-domain representation. LIST Accept defaults override source Accept while explicit Headers prevail.

- Lazy owned full pagination supports body/header continuation, marker fallback on short pages, raw-row caps before local filters and single-page choice. Find checks all candidate rows/pages before returning one exact ID/name; late duplicates/rejected/malformed pages remain errors. Cycles and conflicting/foreign continuation are terminal; standard dictionary next links are a declared Go improvement.

- Only clean native400/403/404 member rejection falls back; default missing requires a completely successful search and strict missing retains no suppressed native rejection cause. Nonobject row/envelope and constructor cls/connection/microversion/_synchronized collisions retain actual page proof. Source response descriptors retain integer/bool/list/null/tag/tenant aliases; malformed accepted member syntax follows seed while valid wrong shapes fail.

- Accepted/rejected Read+Close/cancel/source-changed-and-restored-on-Close failures preserve every cause and physical body/header/status, close once, stop before fallback/missing and never resend. Member result receipts for accepted physical failures hold actual metadata without a fabricated descriptor acknowledgement.
  Tests: `network/v2/extensions/subnetpools/python_records_contracts_test.go:TestPythonSubnetPoolRecordsPhysicalFailuresPreserveAcceptedAndRejectedProof`.
- Native retry cannot change/delete/alias/omit selected network version, inject version when absent, add foreign-service/auth/framing headers or mutate JSON body/Content-Type. One503 proof plus native callback causes survives; ordinary retry headers, exact method/URL/JSON, live token and reauthentication are retained.
  Tests: `network/v2/extensions/subnetpools/python_records_contracts_test.go:TestPythonSubnetPoolRecordsRetriesKeepProtocolBodyAndNativeCauses`.
- Source is captured before callbacks; concrete options snapshot raw JSON/maps/pointers and retained callbacks cannot mutate requests/results. Invalid controls/headers/IDs/descriptors/context/options fail before HTTP and preserve causes. One immutable option supports concurrent requests with independent records/evidence.
  Tests: `network/v2/extensions/subnetpools/python_records_contracts_test.go:TestPythonSubnetPoolRecordsOptionsSourceContextAndConcurrentOwnership`.
- Source Resource.find consumes the explicit microversion for member fetch only; fallback LIST and all its pages keep the captured original selected/default microversion. Ordinary Header options apply to both phases. Fallback retry cannot inject the member-only override when selected version differs or is absent; one original LIST503 body/header/status survives. Direct List/Update per-operation overrides remain effective and never mutate source.
  Tests: `network/v2/extensions/subnetpools/python_records_contracts_test.go:TestPythonSubnetPoolRecordsFindMicroversionIsMemberPhaseOnly`.
- Library-owned PagePolicy.NextTruthy reuses SubnetPool response binary64 truthiness: top-level next1e-400/-1e-400/0.0 are falsey and do not request another page, while truthy numeric1e400/1.0 fail URL decoding with exact whole actual page body/header/status. Normal bool/null next remain falsey; independently yielded row receipts retain their own page snapshots.
  Tests: `network/v2/extensions/subnetpools/python_records_contracts_test.go:TestPythonSubnetPoolRecordsNextUsesBinary64TruthinessAndWholePageProof`.

Python/Go differences:

- The complete declared query/local classification is now SDK-owned through concrete semantic and explicit Body options. id is local rather than a Python query field; queried name/project/default/shared/ip_version/address_scope/tags are server-only. The project response alias does not create a tenant query alias. WithName retains separate hint/local behavior and raw WithQuery is a wire extension, without a caller builder or predicate.
- Individual semantic options are last-wins and bulk canonical presence wins over wire aliases. Only final selected semantic values are validated; same-destination collisions reject even null/empty/equal values. Query arrays encode repeated scalar values with null omission, booleans are lowercase, JSON-number spelling is retained and unknown semantic keys/errors are discarded. Raw prefix spellings are available through explicit Body aliases or raw query, rather than invented semantic property names.
- The five integer response descriptors use exact-integral Go normalization after native decoding. Python int descriptor behavior can truncate fractions, retain bool-as-int values, and default ordinary signed/non-digit strings differently; these conversions are not replicated. Filter values stay uncoerced. Native quota/revision int decoding remains strict, and native prefix floats/strings retain their returned int/float64 or Atoi limits independently of raw comparisons.
- Original timestamps, literal ID/tenant presence and prefix-array null elements drive matching, while returned typed times, native zero strings and []string null-to-empty projection remain unchanged. Native known TenantID is a string and malformed values fail first; no arbitrary tenant-object or precision claim is made. Arrays compare full ordered elements, bools remain distinct from numbers, and Python scalar-to-list wrapping/default/alias projection is not added.
- Whole-page native decoding remains authoritative before raw caps: every row requires all three valid prefix-length fields, so null/empty rows fail on both query and Body lanes. Selected raw integer normalization runs only on consumed fields after that boundary; caps/break can avoid later normalization but cannot hide native type/required-prefix/time errors.
- Native 200/204/300, plural-links continuation and foreign URL following are preserved, including application/json empty-204 EOF. Raw caps count rows like pinned Resource.list but do not infer wire limit; first-page/break stop before continuation and All drops partial results on errors. No RFC-Link parsing, same-origin/fixed-query or synthesized marker policy is added.
- Bounded named mappings cover literal identity/name and JSON-domain attrs, not arbitrary Python mutable Resource instances, Connection/session/cache side effects, resource location calculation or deprecation warnings. location is null; whole Source Resource/Connection parity remains separately tracked.
- Go rejects SDK constructor/control attr collisions rather than allowing caller retargeting, and supplies per-call owned Headers/Microversion and typed list-only pagination conveniences. Raw unknown Body/Find kwargs remain data; unsupported list kwargs are discarded.
- Bulk Go maps are sorted when conflicting canonical/wire alias insertion order matters; individual mutation options preserve order. Local map filter evaluation is sorted. Shared JSON query float repr/Unicode runtime and CPython int-string-limit differences remain explicit common representation bounds.
- Owned pager additionally follows standard links:{next:URL}, which pinned Source dict-to-item loop ignores, and rejects ambiguous/foreign/cyclic links with physical proof. Raw caps, marker-on-short-page and complete find search match Source supported behavior.
- Fixed Neutron27.0.0 policy permits admin/owning-project reader or shared-pool reads and admin/owning-project member updates; is_default update is separately admin-only. Examples omit that admin attr and all implicit defaults. No explicit service-only branch exists. neutron-lib3.21.1 rejects shared/ip_version/tenant_id/id PUT fields; raw SDK attrs stay unpoliced and backend rejection authoritative.

- Canonical/wire Query mapping is pushed down without an invented name hint; non-query declared Body attributes filter loaded descriptors locally with Python loaded JSON equality. Unknown kwargs are discarded before application marshaling. Source max_items/limit raw behavior remains incomplete and is explicitly excluded below.
  Tests: `network/v2/subnets/python_cloud_lists_test.go:TestPythonSubnetListsPushdownLocalLoadedFiltersAndUnknownMarshalDiscard`.
- Proxy._list evaluates JMESPath against the actual opaque Resource.list generator, before Resource control normalization or HTTP. Index/projection/type expressions produce null without HTTP; literals preserve arbitrary JSON; @ returns the same generator, nested to_array keeps it unconsumed, and length errors before HTTP. ListRecords/AllRecords are ordinary resource-only convenience APIs.
  Tests: `network/v2/subnets/python_cloud_lists_test.go:TestPythonSubnetListsProxyGeneratorJMESAndLazyResourceControls`.
- Original bound self/resource_type/cls/session collisions and restored cls/session/base_path/paginated conflicts are eager. Truthy __conflicting_attrs restores ordered kwargs once; restored self/resource_type are discarded as unknown Resource attrs and restored JMES does not replace original expression. Resource headers/base_path/microversion are deferred until actual generator consumption, so an opaque empty literal avoids invalid controls entirely.
  Tests: `network/v2/subnets/python_cloud_lists_test.go:TestPythonSubnetListsOneLevelRestorationBindingAndImmutableOptions`, `network/v2/subnets/python_cloud_lists_test.go:TestPythonSubnetListsProxyGeneratorJMESAndLazyResourceControls`.
- Only explicit empty string base_path produces an empty HTTP0 generator; null selects default, false/zero/list/object fails at consumption. Context/source checks still run before an empty generator returns. Literal same-origin service-relative path policy remains narrower than arbitrary Python formatting.
  Tests: `network/v2/subnets/python_cloud_lists_test.go:TestPythonSubnetListsEmptyPathOnlyNullDefaultAndDelayedCancellation`.
- Per-call microversion None keeps captured default and every other falsey JSON value disables it. Ordinary JSON numeric/string/list/object-key iterable normalization follows KSA conventions including true/1 -> 1.0, [2,4] -> 2.4, v2.4 -> 2.4, latest and major.latest rejection. Python overflow infinity/latest conversion remains unsupported.
  Tests: `network/v2/subnets/python_cloud_lists_test.go:TestPythonSubnetListsMicroversionJSONDomainAndDefaultDisable`.
- Source status100..399, plural singleton objects, required accepted JSON parsing, row constructor/descriptor faults, root links precedence, ignored dictionary next, HTTP/limit/raw-marker continuation use the shared guarded pager. Actual late-page body/read/Close failures preserve original native/ResponseError evidence and callbacks are suppressed only for physical faults; clean token renewal retains captured headers. Dynamic cap exact-boundary semantics remain named gaps.
  Tests: `network/v2/subnets/python_cloud_lists_test.go:TestPythonSubnetListsSourceLinkPrecedenceAndRawMarkerMaxItems`, `network/v2/subnets/python_cloud_lists_test.go:TestPythonSubnetListsMalformedAcceptedPagesKeepPhysicalProof`, `network/v2/subnets/python_cloud_lists_test.go:TestPythonSubnetListsLatePhysicalFaultsNative401AndNoFaultReplay`, `network/v2/subnets/python_cloud_lists_test.go:TestPythonSubnetListsCleanReauthenticationAndCapturedHeaders`.
- The explicit Source-shaped list lane now accepts JSON falsey, true, negative and fractional max_items values, counts raw rows before local filtering, and defers truthy nonnumeric comparison errors until a nonempty raw row; falsey/container and raw limit hints preserve their separate wire forms.
  Tests: `network/v2/subnets/python_raw_max_items_test.go:TestPythonSubnetRawMaxItemsJSONDomainAndDeferredComparison`, `network/v2/subnets/python_raw_max_items_test.go:TestPythonSubnetRawMaxItemsFalseyAndRawLimitHint`, `network/v2/subnets/python_raw_max_items_test.go:TestPythonSubnetRawMaxItemsOpaqueJMESAndOwnedJSON`.
- The cap is checked before each next raw-row constructor. Exact-page cap boundaries may fetch a following page and expose its HTTP/read/Close/context failure; malformed rows beyond the cap are ignored and filtered rows count without refill. Legacy Find/native/typed pager policies remain separate.
  Tests: `network/v2/subnets/python_raw_max_items_test.go:TestPythonSubnetRawMaxItemsExactPageBoundaryObservesNextResponse`, `network/v2/subnets/python_raw_max_items_test.go:TestPythonSubnetRawMaxItemsCountsFilteredRowsAndStopsBeforeConstructor`.
- ProxyListRecords and the cloud list lanes: Raw limit values reach the wire with Requests spelling (False, -1, 1.5, x, repeated values) and Resource.list keeps the original limit for its loop: only a Python-truthy limit performs the marker GET after a nonempty page without a next link, so false/0/0.0/""/null send one GET. base_path="" never enters Resource.list's while loop: no HTTP and no KSA microversion normalization, so an invalid raw microversion is unobserved and the result is empty; a nonempty route still rejects it before HTTP.
  Tests: `network/v2/subnets/python_source_list_limits_test.go:TestPythonSubnetProxyRawLimitTruthiness`, `network/v2/subnets/python_source_list_limits_test.go:TestPythonSubnetProxyEmptyBasePathSkipsVersionNormalization`.

Python/Go differences:

- Go provides SDK-owned WithFilter/WithFilters for complete declared semantic classification, without caller builders or predicates, while WithBodyFilter/WithBodyFilters remain explicit local options and WithQuery remains independent wire data. Python canonical query names win in a bulk map; individual Go options use last-wins and nil/empty bulk replacement clears only semantic state. prefix_length is the semantic Python attribute; explicit Body options also accept raw prefixlen, which is unknown and discarded in the semantic namespace.
- Original page rows preserve unknown nested fields, JSON null elements, timestamp text, missing/null and exact numbers for local comparison; returned values remain native typed Subnets. Go distinguishes bool and number, requires exact ordered arrays/full inner objects and does not perform Python scalar-to-list coercion.
- The untyped Python prefix_length Body descriptor is preserved as raw JSON rather than inventing integer validation or converting response strings into numbers. revision_number is inherited type=int, but the native whole-page int decoder is retained first, so numeric strings/fractional/exponent spellings remain native decode boundaries.
- Native accepted statuses, full-current-page extraction and advertised subnets_links remain intact. max_items caps consumed raw rows locally without an inferred Python limit hint. Native typed List, FindIdentity and the prior four typed Body projections retain their existing behavior.
- Go defines scalar/repeated query encoding explicitly: booleans are lowercase, JSON number text is preserved, null elements are omitted and empty/null query values retain option presence without URL values. Query objects and nested arrays are rejected; no Subnet CSV format or native-q-tag-derived classification is inferred. Unknown semantic attributes are discarded, while explicit unknown Body options are errors and raw wire extensions remain caller-controlled.
- Semantic query name is a server filter rather than a local exact-name predicate. WithName retains its query hint and local exact match; duplicate ownership of the same query or Body destination is rejected across semantic/raw/page/hint/explicit namespaces, including equal values. This is an intentional Go conflict policy rather than permissive Python kwargs merging.
- An explicit semantic clear still requires an audited descriptor. Reserved session/paginated/base_path/allow_unknown_params/microversion/headers/max_items/resource_type/jmespath_filters names are not treated as field payloads. Generation additionally requires a live audited Python source checkout and its recorded AST parser minor version.
- Source-shaped lazy Subnet records and ordinary cloud list/search orchestration are owned by the SDK; existing native List/Resources/Find bindings retain their baseline policies.
- SDK APIs are concrete functional options rather than application builders/interfaces. JSON/individual options preserve source order; unspecified Go map order and cloud literal dictionary key results use deterministic lexical order. UTF8 JSON ownership, cancellation/source validation, typed positive controls and same-origin literal relative paths are explicit Go policies.
- Actual HTTP data is separated from Resource defaults/computed location and JMES literals. LastResponse identifies the last observed yielded resource/error receipt and is not a final-page receipt for empty or fully filtered pages. Native401 preserves ErrOriginal and ErrReauth despite absent Unwrap; physical faults are never presented as clean retry.
- Pinned Source evidence uses CPython3.14.8 actual cloud/Proxy/Resource/KSA code with identified adapter/config/HTTP fixtures, not a live backend or runtime authorization. Independent six fixture sets total696 observations including348 Subnet observations; KSA overflow-edge is header normalization only. Neutron27 stock get_subnet project-read policy and admin-only segment visibility do not guarantee live deployment permissions.
- Raw max_items behavior and exact-boundary cap timing are owned only by the explicit Source-shaped list path; historical typed/native contracts above retain their original boundaries.
- Only standard finite JSON numbers are accepted: NaN, Infinity and overflowing literals such as 1e400 return ErrUnsupported with the original response, where Python's json loads them as floats, and a latest microversion must be written as "latest"; see Deliberate runtime differences in docs/sdk-support-ledger.md.
- Results are immutable owned snapshots rather than Python's mutable Resource with adapter, session, cache and dirty state (SDK-R1/C1/S1); see Deliberate runtime differences in docs/sdk-support-ledger.md.

Python/Go differences:

- Go exposes concrete SDK-owned WithFilter/WithFilters for complete declared classification and WithBodyFilter/WithBodyFilters for explicit local selection, without caller builders/predicates. Raw WithQuery remains a separate wire namespace; ordinary Secret Resources preserves its existing status-query suppression. Typed API.List and manual Fetch/FindIdentity do not acquire semantic options.
- Individual Go options are last-wins by target; bulk canonical query names follow Python precedence and bulk replacement/clear affects only semantic state. Cross-namespace duplicate destinations are errors rather than permissive kwargs merging. A selected null/empty query retains presence without URL values; flat scalar arrays encode repeated values, booleans are lowercase and exact JSON number text is retained. Objects/nested query arrays are rejected and final unknown semantic values are discarded.
- Raw local matching keeps original timestamp spelling and precise JSON values, while returned resources remain native typed Secrets with native time/int/map defaults. Raw fields such as payload, literal id and unknown attributes are not added to the returned native model or exposed as complete HTTP response evidence. Native whole-page decoding may fail on a later modeled field before a raw cap or local mismatch can stop consumption.
- Go distinguishes booleans and numbers and does not apply Python descriptor/scalar coercion. Object filters recursively compare subsets of nonempty actual objects, preserving the Python empty-object truthiness boundary; nonobject actual values are nonmatches rather than potential Python attribute-access errors. Arrays require exact ordered values including full objects inside arrays.
- Passive id uses raw key presence or the full reference; secret_id remains a separate formatted property and never requires a UUID. Go formats only selected secret_id conditions, while Python Resource construction/to_dict can eagerly invoke the formatter on unselected fields. Go URL parsing preserves original percent/path spelling but differs from Python urlsplit for nonnumeric authority ports and leading-control normalization.
- Native HTTP 200/204/300 and top-level next pagination are retained. A bodyless 204 without JSON Content-Type is empty; a JSON-typed empty 204 retains the native EOF parsing error. The native pager can follow foreign advertised URLs and does not acquire the same-origin/fixed-query offset guards used by manual Secret FindIdentity. Local caps stop on consumed raw rows without a Python-inferred server limit; first-page/break stop before continuation and All discards partial results on a terminal error.
- Semantic name is a server condition rather than an automatic exact local predicate; WithName retains the latter. Semantic Body status is exact JSON, whereas common WithStatus retains case-insensitive comparison. The nine source controls are reserved as field names; this does not imply every inherited per-call control is implemented by the ordinary collection.
- Generation additionally requires an independently verified live Python checkout and recorded AST parser minor version. Manifest/accessor/formatter/source/native drift fails closed, and Subnet proof plus existing native identity capability tables remain independent.
- New concrete leaf ListRecords/AllRecords with existing owned metadata models supplement native typed List and Resources.List/All, whose generated/native semantics are unchanged. Complete finite read views are independent of actual raw proof, not mutable Python resource/session/dirty/cache/JMESPath/conflicting-attrs overload.
- Computed location stays null using the existing key-manager projectors; no connection/location I/O or guessed cloud/configured scope. Source canonical-versus-wire alias merge order differs from deterministic existing Go canonical-wire priority, and eager Go HREF parsing has documented Python urlsplit edge differences.
- Finite opts expose max-items/first-page/headers/microversion; free base-path and raw inherited control kwargs are rejected to preserve fixed routes rather than adding caller interface builders. Unknown recognized JSON-domain kwargs discard; WithRecordListQuery reports Go JSON encoding errors.
- Source bool/number equality is retained for ordinary JSON values but shared EqualPythonJSON exact-decimal values deliberately differ from Python binary64 rounding. Source nonobject actual recursive-dict access can raise; Go returns nonmatch.
- Source has no initial offset query mapping for these models, even though stock/native Barbican supports it. Advertised offsets plus explicit-limit marker fallback use the root opt-in guarded shared pager: one cursor family per request, prior cursor dropped on transition and previously seen maximum offset retained. Origin/path/query/limit/cycles/negative cursor guards are explicit Go policy beyond permissive Source merging.
- Raw caps skip trailing unconsumed schema/HREF/constructor errors, unlike native whole-page typed extraction; capped first request supplies the Source limit hint. AllRecords returns nil rows on terminal error, iterator yields earlier rows with the terminal cause.
- Marker scalar IDs follow Source Requests spellings; arrays/objects use a single Python repr rather than Source iterable query expansion. Dictionary-link compatibility is a Go extension. HTTP valid final100..399 is accepted; actual rejected400..599 body Read/Close/source/cancel observations preserve strict physical evidence.
- ListRecords is metadata-only even when content_types.default exists; existing FoundSecretRecord.Payload is nil and no Secret.fetch metadata/payload sequence occurs. Existing Secret Find helper/projector files remain frozen.
