- Ordinary Containers.Resources.List/All classify the entire pinned Container declaration through SDK-owned WithFilter/WithFilters: inherited limit and marker are the two canonical and accepted query names, and all ten non-query Body properties are supported. name is a local original-row condition, not a semantic server query; native ListOpts.Name, raw name queries and the existing WithName hint remain separate. Both the generated leaf and cached Connection.KeyManagerV1 facade use this descriptor without caller builders or predicates.
  Tests: `api/keymanager_container_list_filters_test.go:TestKeyManagerContainerListFiltersEntireDescriptor`, `api/keymanager_container_list_filters_test.go:TestKeyManagerContainerListFiltersLocalNameAndQueryIsolation`, `connectiontest/connection_keymanager_container_filters_test.go:TestConnectionKeyManagerContainerFiltersSharedFacadeAndLocalName`.
- Semantic query scalars encode strings, lowercase booleans and exact JSON-number text; flat scalar arrays retain repeated order and omit null elements. Null and empty arrays retain key presence for collisions. Unknown semantic names, including offset and raw created/updated names, are discarded. Final selected objects/nested arrays or invalid JSON fail lazily, and all nine source list-control names are reserved rather than accepted as field payloads.
  Tests: `api/keymanager_container_list_filters_test.go:TestKeyManagerContainerListFiltersEntireDescriptor`, `api/keymanager_container_list_filters_test.go:TestKeyManagerContainerListFiltersLocalNameAndQueryIsolation`, `api/keymanager_container_list_filters_test.go:TestKeyManagerContainerListFiltersSnapshotsAndLazyPreflight`, `resource/filters_test.go:TestSemanticFiltersCanonicalPrecedencePresenceAndExactQueryEncoding`, `resource/filters_test.go:TestSemanticFiltersRejectSelectedErrorsAndReservedControlsBeforeIteration`.
- Owned options snapshot nested input values and repeated query slices across application, independent iteration and concurrent reuse. Individual target assignments are last-wins; WithFilters replaces or clears only the semantic namespace, leaving raw query and explicit Body state intact. Only final selected semantic values are validated. Same-wire semantic/raw/page-size collisions and same-property semantic/explicit Body collisions fail before HTTP, in either order and even for equal/null values. Local semantic name can coexist with raw name because the destinations are distinct.
  Tests: `api/keymanager_container_list_filters_test.go:TestKeyManagerContainerListFiltersSnapshotsAndLazyPreflight`, `api/keymanager_container_list_filters_test.go:TestKeyManagerContainerListFiltersLocalNameAndQueryIsolation`, `connectiontest/connection_keymanager_container_filters_test.go:TestConnectionKeyManagerContainerFiltersPreflightAndRawNameNamespace`, `resource/filters_test.go:TestSemanticFiltersSnapshotsConcurrentReuseAndServerOnlyName`, `resource/filters_test.go:TestSemanticFiltersValidateOnlyFinalSelectedValues`, `resource/filters_test.go:TestSemanticFiltersNamespaceCollisionsAndIndependentClear`, `resource/filters_test.go:TestSemanticFiltersDescriptorGateAndWireAliasBodyOverlap`.
- All ten local properties project from the original page row after native extraction. created_at and updated_at compare the original created/updated strings, and secret_refs/consumers retain full ordered arrays, unknown nested keys, null elements and exact numbers beyond float64. Missing/null values remain distinct from empty arrays. Objects use recursive subsets: an empty actual object rejects an object filter, while an empty filter matches a nonempty actual object. Numeric decimals compare exactly; boolean/number and string/number types remain distinct.
  Tests: `api/keymanager_container_list_filters_test.go:TestKeyManagerContainerListFiltersEntireDescriptor`, `api/keymanager_container_list_filters_test.go:TestKeyManagerContainerListFiltersPassiveIDsAndRawJSON`, `connectiontest/connection_keymanager_container_filters_test.go:TestConnectionKeyManagerContainerFiltersSharedFacadeAndLocalName`, `resource/body_record_test.go:TestBodyRecordPairingPreservesOriginalRowsAndOwnsFields`, `internal/jsonfilter/filter_test.go:TestMatchFiltersArraysCompareExactly`, `internal/jsonfilter/filter_test.go:TestMatchFiltersExactDecimalNumbersAndHugeExponents`, `internal/jsonfilter/filter_test.go:TestMatchFiltersDeliberatelyDistinguishesBooleansAndNumbers`.
- The id property gives literal raw id presence priority, including null, empty, numeric and object values; only an absent id falls back to the full container_ref. container_id is a separate HREFToUUID-style final-path projection from the original reference spelling, preserving percent escapes and literal Unicode/space, excluding query/fragment and producing an empty string for a trailing slash. Missing/null bypass formatting. Invalid selected formatters are terminal before common local predicates. References remain passive comparisons and never cause follow-up requests or synthesize native model IDs.
  Tests: `api/keymanager_container_list_filters_test.go:TestKeyManagerContainerListFiltersPassiveIDsAndRawJSON`, `connectiontest/connection_keymanager_container_filters_test.go:TestConnectionKeyManagerContainerFiltersSharedFacadeAndLocalName`, `internal/jsonfilter/reference_test.go:TestReferenceLastComponentPassiveSpelling`, `internal/jsonfilter/reference_test.go:TestReferenceLastComponentErrors`.
- Native Container extraction validates the entire current page before raw selectors run, preserving modeled string/array/timestamp and nested SecretRef/ConsumerRef failures even beyond a raw cap. A null row consumed by a Body-filter iterator is invalid; query-only iteration retains the native zero-model null-row result. After native extraction succeeds, a cap or break can skip a later selected null row or invalid formatter. Caps count raw rows before Body/name/status matches without refill or a wire limit hint; first-page and break stop before continuation, and All returns nil on terminal errors.
  Tests: `api/keymanager_container_list_filters_test.go:TestKeyManagerContainerListFiltersControlsAndWholePageFailures`, `resource/body_record_test.go:TestBodyRecordPairingRejectsUnprovenShapeAndCount`, `resource/body_filters_test.go:TestCollectionBodyFiltersRunAfterRawCapsOnBothIteratorPaths`.
- Ordinary listing retains native HTTP 200/204/300 acceptance and rejects 201. A successfully parsed 204 is empty, while an empty 204 advertised as application/json yields native EOF before the emptiness check. Top-level next remains authoritative, including foreign URLs, and matching duplicate rows are returned. Later HTTP/decode/foreign-request failures, cycles and cancellation are terminal. The original ProviderClient, live token, configured headers, current microversion, middleware and reverse prefix remain shared across pages without client replacement.
  Tests: `api/keymanager_container_list_filters_test.go:TestKeyManagerContainerListFiltersNativeSurfaceAndStatusPolicy`, `api/keymanager_container_list_filters_test.go:TestKeyManagerContainerListFiltersPagingAndLiveSource`, `connectiontest/connection_keymanager_container_filters_test.go:TestConnectionKeyManagerContainerFiltersSharedFacadeAndLocalName`, `connectiontest/connection_keymanager_container_filters_test.go:TestConnectionKeyManagerContainerFiltersPreflightAndRawNameNamespace`.
- Body matching precedes common local name/status predicates. Semantic Body status compares exact JSON, whereas WithStatus retains local case-insensitive matching. Both normal and raw-filter Container iterators preserve baseline raw status-query removal and transfer other complete query maps with repeated/nil/empty key ownership. Raw name/offset remain native wire extensions. Typed API.List, Get and explicit-Ref lookup retain their separate contracts, and this unit neither enables Container FindIdentity nor adds detail/reference GETs.
  Tests: `api/keymanager_container_list_filters_test.go:TestKeyManagerContainerListFiltersLocalNameAndQueryIsolation`, `api/keymanager_container_list_filters_test.go:TestKeyManagerContainerListFiltersNativeSurfaceAndStatusPolicy`, `connectiontest/connection_keymanager_container_filters_test.go:TestConnectionKeyManagerContainerFiltersPreflightAndRawNameNamespace`.
- Generation requires the checked-in Container manifest and live audited -openstacksdk-source checkout. Seven file SHA-256 hashes are checked before isolated AST-only extraction and complete manifest comparison, including parser minor version, C3 MRO, inherited Resource._query_mapping and pagination defaults, literal/alternate id access, distinct HREF formatter and list coercion. Twenty-three AST anchors and optional response_accessor/formatter metadata remain separate from the runtime property names. Source/manifest drift fails closed without importing OpenStackSDK; existing Subnet and Secret extraction remain unchanged.
  Tests: `internal/cmd/sdkgen/container_filters_test.go:TestContainerPythonFilterManifestKeepsInheritedQueriesAndPropertyAccessors`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/container_filters_test.go:TestContainerPythonFilterPinnedSourceRequiresInheritedDefaultProofAndIndependentAST`.
- The plan additionally requires the exact native nine-field Container, three-field ListOpts, nested ConsumerRef/SecretRef shapes, decoder, linked-page/extractor contracts and thirteen receiver-qualified native/pagination declaration hashes, including UseNumber and original GetBody pairing. The sole native SecretRef builder is permitted only with its exact value-receiver map[string]any,error signature; its unused implementation is not claimed as a list contract. A real compiled native export-graph regression verifies this method outside results.go. Emission retains both query bridges and prior Secret resource-function ASTs; explicit Body and semantic inventory capabilities remain distinct, Body bindings are seven, semantic bindings three, and twenty native identity opt-ins are unchanged.
  Tests: `internal/cmd/sdkgen/container_filters_test.go:TestContainerBodyFilterNativeContractRejectsNestedModelsDecoderPagerAndSourceDrift`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/container_filters_test.go:TestContainerSemanticFilterEmissionKeepsNameLocalNativePoliciesAndQueryOwnership`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/container_filters_test.go:TestContainerRawPageDependencyLoaderRequiresExactNumbersAndOriginalBody`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/container_filters_test.go:TestContainerBodyFilterAcceptsRealPinnedNativeTypeGraphAndBuilderMethod`.
- Lazy metadata-only ListRecords/AllRecords use complete nullable existing Source projection, flat untyped known values plus absent defaults and independent actual Wire/Envelope/header/status. RequestID remains empty; actual final199 is accepted; selected/default-less versions cause no discovery and default application/json wins configured Accept.
  Tests: `keymanager/v1/containers/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonContainerListRecordsDefaultsAndOwnedResourceEvidence`.
- Only pinned declared mappings reach wire; Container/Order name remains local, Secret name remains server-only, algorithm beats alg including null, unknown offset/invalid unknown values discard. Projected Body filters apply Python ordinary bool/number and nested subset semantics; raw cap before filter and absent/zero limit hint, owned nested Query options.
  Tests: `keymanager/v1/containers/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonContainerListRecordsQueryClassificationAndRawCap`.
- Raw consumed constructor connection/microversion/_synchronized/cls presence rejects null/false after earlier rows with actual accepted200 whole-page receipt; cap stops before trailing collision/invalid HREF; self retained only in Wire and singleton object supported.
  Tests: `keymanager/v1/containers/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonContainerListRecordsConstructorControlsCapsAndSelf`, `keymanager/v1/containers/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonContainerListRecordsRawCapsBeforeDescriptorAndLatePageErrors`.
- Root opt-in shared marker-or-offset pager follows body/RFC Link forward offsets and versioned alias, explicit-limit short-page marker uses retained full passive reference. First page, break and raw cap stop before continuation; public Resource mutation does not change marker routing; foreign/filter/backward/cycle continuations fail with actual page proof.
  Tests: `keymanager/v1/containers/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonContainerListRecordsOffsetBodyHTTPLinkAndMarkerPaging`, `keymanager/v1/containers/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonContainerListRecordsContinuationScopeAndCycles`.
- Per-call headers/version pointers are snapshotted and selected source stays fixed; option callbacks once per iteration, nil/invalid/protected header/negative limit/cap/control conflict and custom callback cancellation fail before any HTTP.
  Tests: `keymanager/v1/containers/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonContainerListRecordsLazyOwnedHeadersVersionAndPreflight`.
- Accepted malformed/null/scalar/bodyless204 responses retain actual ResponseError body/header/status. Clean rejected400/403/404/429/500/503 retain original native expected100..399 codes/method/URL/body/header; late503 discards partial AllRecords; original transport cause survives.
  Tests: `keymanager/v1/containers/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonContainerListRecordsAcceptedSchemasAndNativeFailures`, `keymanager/v1/containers/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonContainerListRecordsRawCapsBeforeDescriptorAndLatePageErrors`.

Python/Go differences:

- Go provides concrete SDK-owned semantic and explicit Body options without caller builders or predicates. Container semantic name is local because the Python class inherits only limit/marker query mapping; native typed Name, raw name query and common WithName remain separate server-hint APIs. Raw name and semantic local name can be combined, while duplicate destinations within one wire/Body namespace fail instead of permissive kwargs merging.
- Individual semantic assignments are last-wins by target and bulk replacement/clear affects only semantic state. Selected null/empty query values retain presence without URL values, flat scalar arrays emit repeated values, booleans use lowercase and JSON-number text is retained. Selected objects/nested query arrays are rejected, and unknown final semantic names are discarded; reserved source controls do not imply every inherited per-call control is implemented.
- Local comparisons retain original dates, precise numbers and full nested array objects, but results remain native typed Containers with nine modeled fields, parsed time.Time values and native string reference structs. Unknown original/nested fields and raw id are not added to returned models or exposed as full HTTP evidence. Native whole-page decoding can fail before a raw cap or local mismatch stops consumption; Python list descriptors may instead coerce scalars into one-element lists.
- Go distinguishes booleans from numbers and does not apply Python property coercion. Recursive object subsets preserve the Python empty-actual-object truthiness boundary, while nonobject actual values are safe nonmatches instead of potential Python attribute-access errors. Arrays compare complete ordered values, including all object keys inside arrays.
- Passive id uses literal key presence or the entire reference, while container_id remains a distinct formatted property without UUID validation, unescaping or follow-up HTTP. Go formats only selected conditions; Python construction/to_dict can invoke formatters on unselected properties. Go parsing preserves original escaped path spelling but differs from Python urlsplit for some malformed authority ports and leading-control normalization.
- Native HTTP 200/204/300 and top-level next pagination remain, including foreign advertised URLs and native JSON EOF for application/json empty204. This list unit does not acquire manual Secret Finder same-origin/fixed-query guards. Local caps stop on consumed raw rows without an inferred server limit; first-page/break stop before continuation and terminal All errors discard partial results.
- Semantic status is exact original JSON; common WithStatus is case-insensitive and local. Normal and raw-filter iterators preserve Container status-query suppression. Existing native typed List/Get/explicit-Ref/CRUD behavior and identity opt-ins remain separate; this ordinary list layer adds no Container heuristic FindIdentity or update endpoint claim.
- Source classification is guarded by an independently re-extracted live Python manifest plus native decoder/pager/type contracts. Python parser-version and source/native/manifest drift fail closed. The exact unused SecretRef builder signature is a compatibility guard, rather than a claim to map its implementation into ordinary listing.
- New concrete leaf ListRecords/AllRecords with existing owned metadata models supplement native typed List and Resources.List/All, whose generated/native semantics are unchanged. Complete finite read views are independent of actual raw proof, not mutable Python resource/session/dirty/cache/JMESPath/conflicting-attrs overload.
- Computed location stays null using the existing key-manager projectors; no connection/location I/O or guessed cloud/configured scope. Source canonical-versus-wire alias merge order differs from deterministic existing Go canonical-wire priority, and eager Go HREF parsing has documented Python urlsplit edge differences.
- Finite opts expose max-items/first-page/headers/microversion; free base-path and raw inherited control kwargs are rejected to preserve fixed routes rather than adding caller interface builders. Unknown recognized JSON-domain kwargs discard; WithRecordListQuery reports Go JSON encoding errors.
- Source bool/number equality is retained for ordinary JSON values but shared EqualPythonJSON exact-decimal values deliberately differ from Python binary64 rounding. Source nonobject actual recursive-dict access can raise; Go returns nonmatch.
- Source has no initial offset query mapping for these models, even though stock/native Barbican supports it. Advertised offsets plus explicit-limit marker fallback use the root opt-in guarded shared pager: one cursor family per request, prior cursor dropped on transition and previously seen maximum offset retained. Origin/path/query/limit/cycles/negative cursor guards are explicit Go policy beyond permissive Source merging.
- Raw caps skip trailing unconsumed schema/HREF/constructor errors, unlike native whole-page typed extraction; capped first request supplies the Source limit hint. AllRecords returns nil rows on terminal error, iterator yields earlier rows with the terminal cause.
- Marker scalar IDs follow Source Requests spellings; arrays/objects use a single Python repr rather than Source iterable query expansion. Dictionary-link compatibility is a Go extension. HTTP valid final100..399 is accepted; actual rejected400..599 body Read/Close/source/cancel observations preserve strict physical evidence.

Projected Body filters apply Python ordinary bool/number and nested subset semantics; raw cap before filter and absent/zero limit hint, owned nested Query options.
  Tests: `keymanager/v1/orders/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonOrderListRecordsQueryClassificationAndRawCap`.

  Tests: `keymanager/v1/orders/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonOrderListRecordsConstructorControlsCapsAndSelf`, `keymanager/v1/orders/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonOrderListRecordsRawCapsBeforeDescriptorAndLatePageErrors`.
- Root opt-in shared marker-or-offset pager follows body/RFC Link forward offsets and versioned alias, explicit-limit short-page marker uses retained full passive reference. First page, break and raw cap stop before continuation; public Resource mutation does not change marker routing; foreign/filter/backward/cycle continuations fail with actual page proof.
  Tests: `keymanager/v1/orders/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonOrderListRecordsOffsetBodyHTTPLinkAndMarkerPaging`, `keymanager/v1/orders/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonOrderListRecordsContinuationScopeAndCycles`.

- Accepted malformed/null/scalar/bodyless204 responses retain actual ResponseError body/header/status. Clean rejected400/403/404/429/500/503 retain original native expected100..399 codes/method/URL/body/header; late503 discards partial AllRecords; original transport cause survives.
  Tests: `keymanager/v1/orders/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonOrderListRecordsAcceptedSchemasAndNativeFailures`, `keymanager/v1/orders/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonOrderListRecordsRawCapsBeforeDescriptorAndLatePageErrors`.

Python/Go differences:

- Go uses concrete SDK-owned semantic and explicit Body options. Semantic name is the inherited top-level raw property and never Meta.Name; raw name is a separate wire extension. Because the native Order model has no top-level Name, common WithName and Name Ref lookup remain unsupported and no heuristic FindIdentity is introduced.
- Individual semantic options use last-wins targets and bulk replace/clear affects only semantic state; final selected values alone are validated. Same-destination wire/Body namespace collisions fail instead of permissive kwargs merging. Null/empty query values retain presence without URL values, flat scalar arrays become repeated values, booleans are lowercase, JSON numbers retain text and unknown semantic keys are discarded. Reserved controls do not imply inherited per-call controls are all implemented.
- Comparisons retain raw timestamps and metadata/unknown values, but results remain native Order13/Meta6 models with parsed timestamps, strict integer/string fields and no top-level raw name/id/unknown metadata projection. Whole-page native decoding can fail before a cap or local mismatch stops iteration. Python meta=dict conversion can replace nonobject values with an empty dict; native Meta decoding instead rejects incompatible objects/scalars first.
- Exact decimal JSON matching distinguishes bools from numbers and strings from numbers. Recursive subsets preserve Python empty-actual-object truthiness; nonobject actual values are nonmatches rather than potential attribute-access errors. Arrays require exact ordered values and full nested object keys rather than recursive object subsets inside arrays.
- Literal/full order_ref id, order_id and secret_id stay distinct passive properties. Only selected Go formatters run, whereas Python construction/to_dict may format unselected references. Go preserves percent/path spelling without UUID validation or HTTP, but url.Parse rejects some authorities accepted by Python urlsplit and leading-control normalization differs.
- The native identity callback selects its own OrderRef and still returns a path component, separate from passive semantic id. Explicit-ID routes were already fixed to the caller ID; the callback change is not presented as a reproduced HTTP retargeting fix or as Python full-reference input/Resource identity parity.
- Native200/204/300, top-level-next continuation and foreign URL following remain. application/json empty204 can fail with native EOF. Local caps do not infer a Python wire limit; first-page/break stop before continuation and All discards partial results on terminal failure. No same-origin/fixed-query or synthetic marker/offset continuation policy is added.
- Native typed List/Get/Create/Delete and explicit-ID policies remain independent of the semantic list lane. Raw status queries keep their previous suppression; semantic Body status is exact JSON while common WithStatus is case-insensitive. Source/native/manifest drift fails closed, without extending these list tests into unsupported update endpoint claims.
- New concrete leaf ListRecords/AllRecords with existing owned metadata models supplement native typed List and Resources.List/All, whose generated/native semantics are unchanged. Complete finite read views are independent of actual raw proof, not mutable Python resource/session/dirty/cache/JMESPath/conflicting-attrs overload.
- Computed location stays null using the existing key-manager projectors; no connection/location I/O or guessed cloud/configured scope. Source canonical-versus-wire alias merge order differs from deterministic existing Go canonical-wire priority, and eager Go HREF parsing has documented Python urlsplit edge differences.
- Finite opts expose max-items/first-page/headers/microversion; free base-path and raw inherited control kwargs are rejected to preserve fixed routes rather than adding caller interface builders. Unknown recognized JSON-domain kwargs discard; WithRecordListQuery reports Go JSON encoding errors.
- Source bool/number equality is retained for ordinary JSON values but shared EqualPythonJSON exact-decimal values deliberately differ from Python binary64 rounding. Source nonobject actual recursive-dict access can raise; Go returns nonmatch.
- Source has no initial offset query mapping for these models, even though stock/native Barbican supports it. Advertised offsets plus explicit-limit marker fallback use the root opt-in guarded shared pager: one cursor family per request, prior cursor dropped on transition and previously seen maximum offset retained. Origin/path/query/limit/cycles/negative cursor guards are explicit Go policy beyond permissive Source merging.
- Raw caps skip trailing unconsumed schema/HREF/constructor errors, unlike native whole-page typed extraction; capped first request supplies the Source limit hint. AllRecords returns nil rows on terminal error, iterator yields earlier rows with the terminal cause.
- Marker scalar IDs follow Source Requests spellings; arrays/objects use a single Python repr rather than Source iterable query expansion. Dictionary-link compatibility is a Go extension. HTTP valid final100..399 is accepted; actual rejected400..599 body Read/Close/source/cancel observations preserve strict physical evidence.

Python/Go differences:

- Go uses concrete WithFilter/WithFilters and explicit Body options with frozen values rather than Python keyword arguments and mutable Resource objects. Semantic property names and raw response keys remain separate: revision_number reads revision, deprecated tenant_id stays local and project_id stays query-mapped. Explicit Body bulk aliases reject; semantic bulk query canonical keys win by presence, while individual options are last-wins.
- Selected values come from original JSON after native whole-page decoding. Unknown Gateway/Route keys, numeric precision, timestamp spelling and null array members can be matched without changing native model projections. Known native strings/bools/ints/time/nested fields may reject first, including malformed rows beyond a raw cap. Body-filter null rows reject while query-only native zero rows remain accepted.
- Response-only boolean truthiness preserves missing/null and uses exact decimal zero/nonzero; tiny nonzero values remain true where Python float parsing can underflow. Caller conditions retain their types, so Go boolean/number equality differs from Python. Exact-integral response policy rejects fraction/bool/container coercion and accepts signed integer strings; Python can truncate floats, preserve bool-as-int equality or return zero for unsupported numeric strings.
- Python list descriptors wrap non-list values and dict descriptors coerce nonnull nonobjects to empty dictionaries. The Go raw lane does neither; arrays compare complete ordered elements and standalone dictionaries use recursive subset matching only on nonempty objects. Original timestamp spelling is compared even when instants are equivalent.
- Known query arrays become repeated URL values rather than arbitrary Python container formatting. Nil/empty arrays preserve option-key presence while omitting values. Unknown semantic keys are discarded; raw WithQuery remains an explicit wire extension with no automatic alias/body classification.
- Raw local max_items omits Python Resource.list limit hints. Native linked-page codes, full-page decoding, JSON-empty204 EOF and advertised foreign continuation behavior remain unchanged. No per-call base-path/session/header/microversion or deprecated JMESPath field condition is inferred from reserved source controls.
- Go accepts string identity and immutable owned JSON records; mutable Python Resource/Connection/cache/Adapter lifecycle and computed location remain separately tracked. RouterReadRecord aliases the existing RouterActionRecord.
- JSON-domain concrete options and deterministic bulk-map ordering replace arbitrary Python runtime kwargs. Deprecated JMESPath, allow_unknown_params and __conflicting_attrs restoration plus SDK-owned constructor/route/session controls are explicitly bounded preflight errors, not full runtime parity.
- General local numeric equality deliberately uses shared exact-decimal JSON rather than all CPython binary64 rounding/nonfinite/custom-object comparisons. Descriptor integer float cast and bool/numeric-next truthiness use binary64; Unicode integer-string tables use Unicode16 and do not emulate configurable Python string digit caps.
- Completed status200..399 is supported; arbitrary adapters final informational100..199 are outside this Go completion profile. Actual400..599 and all physical/Source/cancel causes retain actual receipts.
- Continuations require same origin, same fixed routers collection and original filters. Dictionary link forms and exact versioned /v2.0/routers alias are explicit Go conveniences; foreign/rewrite/cycle failures preserve actual whole-page proof.
- Safe Header controls are explicit Go policy. Per-call Find version affects only member GET and List version applies all pages; explicit empty version omits Header and selected client remains untouched.
- Neutron27 stock get_router permits project ADMIN_OR_PROJECT_READER; distributed/ha attributes have separate ADMIN branches. Sparse user responses retain Source missing null; this implementation does not elevate access or prove live deployment authorization/extensions/backend support.

- Pinned direct Proxy.security_groups delegates query to SecurityGroup Resource.list. Ordinary SecurityGroups.Resources.List/All and API.All provide SDK-owned WithFilter/WithFilters classification and explicit WithBodyFilter predicates without caller builders or callbacks. Leaf and cached Connection.Network().API/NetworkV2 facades share the existing native service/provider client; no handwritten top-level SecurityGroup collection or public ResourceAdapter is added.

- Seventeen canonical query attributes and twenty-one accepted canonical/wire spellings include inherited limit/marker/tags and shared/tag aliases. Both project_id and tenant_id are explicit separate server query keys; the response project alias does not collapse them. Inherited revision_number, stateful, is_shared, id and name are also query-only. Only created_at, updated_at and security_group_rules are local Body properties.

- Semantic bulk canonical query names take precedence by presence over wire aliases even for null/false/empty values. Individual same-wire options are last-wins. Scalar arrays retain repeated URL values and exact number spelling while omitting null members; nil/empty arrays omit URL values without losing option-key presence. Query objects/nested arrays and nine source controls reject lazily. Unknown semantic names are discarded before value validation.

- Semantic query conditions conflict before HTTP with raw query/page-size/name hints for the same wire key; semantic and explicit Body conditions for the same raw field also conflict even when values agree. WithStatus remains unsupported because the native model has no Status field.

- All three local fields use original JSON without response-only boolean or integer normalization. - Original rule arrays retain unknown nested keys, precise large JSON numbers and complete ordered elements after successful native decoding. Whole-array objects compare fully rather than as recursive subsets; standalone object filters use the existing subset policy on nonempty actual objects. Python/Go differences:

- Go uses concrete frozen WithFilter/WithFilters and explicit Body options instead of Python keyword arguments and mutable Resource descriptors. Canonical query keys win by presence in bulk maps; individual options are last-wins. Both tenant_id/project_id remain query attributes despite the response alias. Unknown semantic names are discarded, while raw WithQuery remains an explicit wire extension.
- Original JSON is available only after successful native whole-page decoding. Known group/rule strings, bools, ints, nested arrays and timestamps can reject before filtering, including rows past a cap. Unknown rule members retain exact numbers and null array members for comparison without changing the returned native zero/projection policy.
- Python list descriptors wrap scalar values, while the Go raw lane does not. Arrays compare full ordered elements; standalone dictionaries retain subset matching on nonempty actual objects. Go bool/number equality is distinct from Python, and original date spellings are compared literally. All three local descriptors are raw JSON; queried int/bool descriptors introduce no new local coercion.
- Known semantic query arrays become repeated URL values rather than arbitrary Python container formatting. Nil/empty arrays preserve option-key presence while omitting values. Reserved source controls are not interpreted as per-call session/base-path/header/microversion or JMESPath options.
- Raw-row caps omit Python Resource.list wire-limit hints and stop before continuation. Native whole-page extraction, native linked pagination including foreign following, and JSON-empty204 EOF remain unchanged. Body-filter null rows reject; query-only native zero rows remain accepted.
- New Source-shaped record list is sibling to existing native typed List/All, generated Resources classifier and owned FindRecord; no existing API is recounted or changed. Private profile reuses existing verified12-field canonical/wire inventory and shared neutronfind Engine rather than adding a decoder/transport framework.
- Go concrete JSON options/owned Resource/Wire/Envelope snapshots provide the reviewed named list boundary; Python mutable Resource/class/subclass/cache/dirty/same-object/session and Connection current-location synthesis remain independent SDK-R1/C1/S1 foundations. Location is the explicit owned null view, not inferred cloud/auth-config facts.
- Unknown Source list kwargs including Header-only values are discarded before JSON transport encoding. Raw Source session/base_path/resource_type/constructor/control replacement, __conflicting_attrs restoration and deprecated JMESPath expression runtime are explicitly rejected and documented shared framework differences; no silent expression success or complete Python Proxy parity claim.
- Sequential predicates and raw response constructor aliases preserve JSON dictionary order; bulk maps/new callback keys are sorted. Numeric equality uses common exact decimal JSON plus bool-number behavior; decimal truthiness uses CPython binary64 underflow and shared integer descriptor conversion, not arbitrary Python runtime rounding/nonfinite/custom objects.
- Go limit/cap require nonnegative machine integers and marker one nonblank UTF8 value. Safe fixed origin/collection/filter/protocol route and exact /v2.0/security-groups alias restrict Source broad continuation retargeting; dictionary links are Gocompatibility. Raw cap counts excluded rows and explicit cap/singlepage observes only that range.
- Source LIST Header descriptor comes from row canonical exact if_match/case-insensitive wire if-match, not actual wholepage HTTP headers. Actual Header and Wire remain separate; uppercase IF_MATCH remains unknown Body extension. Computed location ignores raw wire spoofing under SourceFindPolicy.
- Pinned Source accepts status<400; Go deliberately handles final200..399 and rejects informational100..199 with actual native proof. UTF8 is strict versus Requests replacement decoding. Native OkCodes hooks can reach retry2 but final original acceptance remains enforced; no blanket preflight rejection claim.
- Fixed Neutron27 policy permits project-reader or shared-security-group visibility paths and stock collection controller filters authorization per row. SDK adds no client authorization gate or all-project permission inference. Fixture/race/main build evidence is distinct from authenticated deployment policy/endpoints, and write/admin API parity is not part of this list row.

  Tests: `api/trunk_list_filters_test.go:TestTrunkListFiltersPagingLiveSourceAndNativeStatus`, `connectiontest/connection_trunk_filters_test.go:TestConnectionTrunkFiltersSharedClientAndDistinctQueryBodyNamespaces`, `connectiontest/connection_trunk_filters_test.go:TestConnectionTrunkFiltersPreflightFinalStatusAndNativeSurfaces`.
- Ordinary typed native List/Get/FindIdentity/explicit Ref and existing tags/subport actions remain independent from semantic List filters. Raw wire id/tenant/status/vendor/revision values remain intact in ordinary and Body lanes; native nested full models and action envelopes are returned without fabricating response metadata or a ResourceAdapter API.
  Tests: `api/trunk_list_filters_test.go:TestTrunkListFiltersNativeAndIdentitySurfaceIsolation`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/trunk_filters_test.go:TestTrunkFilterEmissionOwnsBothQueryLanesAndPreservesPriorTenBindings`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/trunk_filters_test.go:TestTrunkBodyFiltersAcceptActualCompiledPlainNestedAndRequestMethodGraph`.
- Generation fails closed without live sourceSHA6/fresh completeAST35 equality and schema/hash proof.20 reachable native declarations (leaf10/root2/pager8) plus exactly one resourcePath=trunks constant guard builder/result/plain nested model/inherited pager/UseNumber/codes/route drift. Both iterator query maps deep-copy repeated/nil values; prior ten manifest bytes and emitted function ASTs remain unchanged.
  Tests: `internal/cmd/sdkgen/trunk_filters_test.go:TestTrunkPythonManifestSeparatesQuerySubportsFromIndependentJSONIdentity`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/trunk_filters_test.go:TestTrunkBodyNativeSchemaGuardsPlainModelsBuilderAndInheritedContinuation`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/trunk_filters_test.go:TestTrunkFilterEmissionOwnsBothQueryLanesAndPreservesPriorTenBindings`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/trunk_filters_test.go:TestTrunkPythonPinnedSourceRequiresLiveSixSHAAndThirtyFiveASTProof`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/trunk_filters_test.go:TestTrunkDependenciesGuardInheritedLinksWholePageNumbersCodesAndRoute`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/trunk_filters_test.go:TestTrunkBodyFiltersAcceptActualCompiledPlainNestedAndRequestMethodGraph`.
- Lazy generator and complete AllRecords use the original selected service without GET/identity enrichment; repeated ID/name rows remain separate. AllRecords discards partial rows on late page/HTTP/cancellation/source errors.
  Tests: `network/v2/extensions/trunks/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonTrunkListRecordLateErrorsDiscardAllAndDuplicatesStayRows`, `network/v2/extensions/trunks/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonTrunkListRecordOptionOwnershipLazyReuseAndPreflight`.
- Reviewed Trunk Resource+TagMixin effective10 Body descriptors reused directly: missingnull/tags[], explicitnull, project_id alias absence, ordered canonical/wire alias overwrite, CPython boolean truthiness, untyped nested subport list elements and unknown extension/timestamp/raw precision; no NetworkResource revision/Header descriptors.
  Tests: `network/v2/extensions/trunks/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonTrunkListRecordDescriptorsSubportsTagsAndIndependentProof`.
- Exact14 server declarations and tag aliases use canonical query presence before wire aliases includingnull; Source LIST fields values remain original. Unknown kwargs discard before value serialization/validation. id/tenant_id alone are declared nonquery local Body filters.
  Tests: `network/v2/extensions/trunks/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonTrunkListRecordDeclaredQueryAliasesAndUnknownDiscard`, `network/v2/extensions/trunks/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonTrunkListRecordLocalFiltersAliasesAndOrderedFailure`.
- Ordered local recursive dict filtering keeps Source falsey/empty-dict/truthy-scalar/mapping.get error and bool-number equality; decimal truthiness is CPython binary64 while local comparison retains documented exact-decimal JSON profile.
  Tests: `network/v2/extensions/trunks/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonTrunkListRecordLocalFiltersAliasesAndOrderedFailure`, `network/v2/extensions/trunks/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonTrunkListRecordConcurrentSnapshotsAndPaginationTruthiness`.
- Raw-row cap precedes filters and avoids unconsumed bad rows/next; first-page hint, explicitzero-limit/cap, paginated truthiness, consumerbreak and short-page retained Wire marker are owned by existing REST paging. Mutating Resource IDs never changes cursor.
  Tests: `network/v2/extensions/trunks/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonTrunkListRecordRawCapMarkerAndConsumerStop`, `network/v2/extensions/trunks/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonTrunkListRecordConcurrentSnapshotsAndPaginationTruthiness`.
- Source plural/top-level/HTTP/relative-versioned continuations and dictionary compatibility use fixed origin/collection/filter/cursor guards. Top-level next binary64 underflow terminates with actual page preserved; truthy nonstrings and retargeting failures retain whole-page receipt.
  Tests: `network/v2/extensions/trunks/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonTrunkListRecordPagingRepresentationsAndSourceNextTruthiness`, `network/v2/extensions/trunks/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonTrunkListRecordFixedContinuationGuardsAndWholePageReceipt`.
- Go acceptedfinal200..399, representation-required204 and no ignored ordinary LIST400/403/404. Malformed/missing/null/scalar rows and cls/connection/microversion/_synchronized existing-constructor collisions fail with actual acceptedstatus/body/header proof. Source final<400 informational boundary is documented.
  Tests: `network/v2/extensions/trunks/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonTrunkListRecordMalformedConstructorStatusAndPermissionProof`.
- Concrete map/raw/pointer/order/header snapshots and per-iteration capture beforecallbacks are reusable and concurrent. Retained callback state cannot mutate active request. Source/API replacement, nil/canceled context, negative controls, malformed known JSON, unsafe keys and unsupported route/session/JMESPath/unknown-parameter controls fail beforeHTTP. Known JSON builder failures add ErrInvalidOption while preserving caller marshal causes before physical HTTP.
  Tests: `network/v2/extensions/trunks/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonTrunkListRecordOptionOwnershipLazyReuseAndPreflight`, `network/v2/extensions/trunks/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonTrunkListRecordConcurrentSnapshotsAndPaginationTruthiness`, `network/v2/extensions/trunks/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonTrunkListRecordKnownMarshalErrorsPreflightAndUnknownDiscard`.
- Standalone explicit microversion governs every page and empty removes versionheader; matching configured header is replaced only on ownedcopy. LIST Accept defaultsjson with explicit safe override. Retry route/body/header/absence/version guards preserve native rejection/custom cause; clean retry and reauthentication retain live token.
  Tests: `network/v2/extensions/trunks/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonTrunkListRecordExplicitAndAbsentVersionsAcrossPages`, `network/v2/extensions/trunks/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonTrunkListRecordNativeRetryOwnershipAndCleanReauthentication`.
- Physical accepted200 and rejected400/401/403/404/409/429/500/503 mixed Read/Close/customCancel/stickySource errors preserve all causes, actualreceipt, exactlyoneClose/HTTP and no retry/reauth replay under willing callbacks. Unauthorized response follows the actual native ErrUnableToReauthenticate.ErrOriginal receipt and ErrReauth physical/source/cancel causes; the native error intentionally has no Unwrap.
  Tests: `network/v2/extensions/trunks/python_list_contracts_test.go:TestPythonTrunkListRecordPhysicalMixedFaultsNoReplayAndActualReceipt`.

Python/Go differences:

- Go uses frozen concrete WithFilter/WithFilters and explicit Body options instead of Python kwargs and mutable descriptors. Bulk canonical keys win by presence and individual options are last-wins. Raw WithQuery remains an independent explicit wire extension; unknown semantic names discard. Trunk project query and deprecated tenant local Body stay separate.
- Original JSON comparison occurs only after native whole-page extraction. Known string/bool/int/nested Subport/time fields can reject before matching, including later rows past a cap. Returned models remain native projections; original local id/tenant spelling/presence does not expand native model permissiveness.
- All two local descriptors are untyped JSON: no bool/int/list formatter is added. Go does not coerce caller JSON values and distinguishes bool from numbers; Python equality differs. Dictionary subset and ordered-array machinery is shared but native known local strings limit which returned domains reach matching. Queried sub_ports/is_admin_state_up response coercion does not become local filtering.
- Semantic query arrays become repeated scalar URL values rather than arbitrary Python container formatting; nil/empty arrays retain key presence while omitting values. Reserved source controls do not expose caller session/headers/microversion/base_path or JMESPath overrides.
- Go raw cap counts before filtering like Python but omits its limit hint and stops continuation immediately. Native whole-page decoding/HTTP200204300/foreign following/inherited links.next and JSON-empty204 EOF remain. Body-consumed null rows reject; query-only native zero rows remain.
- Concrete immutable TrunkListRecord is an alias of existing TrunkFindRecord and reuses its reviewed Body/query inventory through shared Source opt-in Engine.List. It does not complete mutable Python Resource/cache/dirty/session/subclass/Connection lifecycles or independently change earlier Get/Find/CRUD/Subport mappings.
- Each list iteration physically observes pages, retaining live selected provider auth. Pinned optional Proxy cache_enabled GET caching/invalidation remains the separate shared session/cache foundation; no response cache or cache parity is claimed for this finite named record API.
- Python kwargs become concrete functional options with snapshotted JSON and sorted bulk Go map keys. Sequential query/local filter order is retained; unknown kwargs discard before serialization. allow_unknown_params/JMESPath/conflicting-attrs/route/session/runtime constructor controls are explicit preflighterrors, not an unrestricted Python adapter surface.
- Public Trunk sub_ports/tag list descriptors preserve scalar wrapping/null and untyped nested JSON. Gophercloud native Subport typed extraction, timestamps and revision remain independently callable native surfaces. Raw extension unknown values remain separate actual Wire and Resource JSON; plain/computed/control shadowing and automatic Connection current_location are not full Python equivalence.
- Source integer versus bool interoperability is retained by shared exact-decimal equality plus bool-number matching; scalar fraction/exponent truthiness uses binary64. Full CPython float rounding/nonfinite/custom-object comparison is not claimed.
- Common Engine completion range is final200..399. Source exceptions.raise_from_response accepts final<400, so exposed informational100..199 representations are outside this Go profile; ordinary clients consume interim1xx. Every rejected400..599 actualphysical cause remains observable.
- Same-origin fixed-collection/filter continuation and one nonempty Wire marker query value intentionally restrict Python continuation retargeting. Dictionary links are explicit compatibility. Caps/single-page observe only their declared scope; AllRecords discards partials on late failure rather than claiming unseen-page success.
- Trunk has no class maximum microversion: no discovery is introduced and captured selected/absent version remains. Safe explicit Headers/microversion options own LIST policy and do not grant project/admin authority. Stock Neutron27 project ADMIN_OR_PROJECT_READER classifies the list as core user; live deployment policy remains actual server enforcement.
- Trunk List opts StopFaultRetryCallbacks explicitly and independently of shared Source projection policy. Gophercloud unauthorized reauthentication guard errors retain receipt/cause in ErrOriginal/ErrReauth public fields, as v2.15.0 intentionally does not Unwrap those two independent errors.
- Go fixes the SDK-owned location view to null while preserving actual values in Wire. Source Computed location may accept explicit row kwargs in LIST construction; mutable Connection-derived location remains unresolved under SDK-R1.

### `python:placement/v1/delete_allocation`

Status: `go_mapping`.

Go API:

- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/placement/v1/allocations.API.Delete`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/resource.IgnoreMissing`

- At placement 1.38 Delete sends DELETE allocations/{consumer} and succeeds on 204.
  Tests: `placement/v1/allocations/python_parity_test.go:TestPythonAllocationProxyCalls`.
- Delete reports HTTP 404 as a native gophercloud.ErrUnexpectedResponseCode.
  Tests: `placement/v1/allocations/python_parity_test.go:TestPythonAllocationDeleteMissing`.
- Wrapping the direct delete in resource.IgnoreMissing turns a plain HTTP 404 into success like Python's default ignore_missing=True, keeps other failures such as 409 as errors, and leaves the DELETE request unchanged.
  Tests: `resource/missing_test.go:TestIgnoreMissingDropsOnlyPlain404`, `placement/v1/allocations/python_ignore_missing_test.go:TestPythonDeleteIgnoreMissingAllocation`.

Python/Go differences:

- Python negotiates min(Resource._max_microversion, server maximum) per call unless the session sets a default; Go sends the service client's Microversion string unchanged, so the caller builds a client with the matching value. Python pins 1.38.
- Python accepts a Resource instance or an ID string; Go takes the ID or name string.
- Python ignore_missing=True is the default; Go selects it by wrapping the call in resource.IgnoreMissing, and the bare call returns the 404 like ignore_missing=False.

### `python:block_storage/v3/delete_volume`

Status: `go_mapping`.

Go API:

- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage/v3/volumes.API.Remove`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage/v3/volumes.API.Delete`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage/v3/volumes.WithDeleteQuery`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/blockstorage/v3/volumes.API.ForceDelete`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/resource.ID`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/resource.WithMissingError`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/resource.IgnoreMissing`

- Remove ignores 404 by default and WithMissingError reports NotFound; Delete with WithDeleteQuery reproduces Python's cascade=False&force=False (or True) query and ForceDelete posts os-force_delete for the pre-3.23 force path.
  Tests: `blockstorage/v3/volumes/python_parity_test.go:TestPythonVolumeDeleteDefaultsCascadeForceAndMissing`.
- Wrapping the direct delete in resource.IgnoreMissing turns a plain HTTP 404 into success like Python's default ignore_missing=True, keeps other failures such as 409 as errors, and leaves the DELETE request unchanged.
  Tests: `resource/missing_test.go:TestIgnoreMissingDropsOnlyPlain404`, `blockstorage/v3/volumes/python_ignore_missing_test.go:TestPythonDeleteIgnoreMissingVolume`.

Python/Go differences:

- At microversion 3.23 or later Python always sends cascade and force as query parameters; Remove sends none and relies on the same server defaults.
- Python chooses the force action or query from microversion support; the Go caller chooses ForceDelete or the force query explicitly.
- ForceDelete sends {"os-force_delete": ""} where Python sends null.
- Python ignore_missing=True maps to Remove(ctx, resource.ID(id)) and ignore_missing=False to resource.WithMissingError(); the plain generated Delete returns the 404 error.
- Python sends min(resource _max_microversion, server maximum) or the session default; Go sends the one microversion configured or negotiated on the Connection client.
- Python ignore_missing=True is the default; Go selects it by wrapping the call in resource.IgnoreMissing, and the bare call returns the 404 like ignore_missing=False.

Python/Go differences:

- Python ignore_missing=True is the default; Go selects it by wrapping the call in resource.IgnoreMissing, and the bare call returns the 404 like ignore_missing=False.

## Usage examples

Examples use the surrounding guide's service object and context unless they declare a complete program. Python examples describe the pinned comparison API.

```go
resource.ID("server-id")
resource.Name("web-01")
resource.ID(server.ID)
```

```go
for server, err := range service.Servers.List(ctx, resource.WithPageSize(100)) {
    if err != nil { return err }
    fmt.Println(server.ID)
    if shouldStop { break }
}
```

```go
node, err := service.Nodes.Resources.Wait(ctx, resource.ID("node-uuid"), "power on",
    resource.WithStatusAttribute("power_state"),
    resource.WithFailureStates("error"),
    resource.WithUnlimitedWait(),
    resource.WithProgressCallback(func(progress int) { fmt.Println(progress) }))
if err != nil { return err }
_ = node
```

```go
package example

import (
    "context"
    "fmt"

    "github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/resource"
)

func PrintFirstPage[T any](ctx context.Context, collection *resource.Collection[T]) error {
    for value, err := range collection.List(ctx,
        resource.WithMaxItems(5), resource.WithPaginated(false)) {
        if err != nil { return err }
        fmt.Println(value)
    }
    return nil
}
```
